## The Serpent's Spine Guide

A guide to progression, keying, flagging, and access within The Serpent's Spine expansion.

| [![](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/mini-sunderock-geyser.jpg)](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/sunderock-geyser.jpg) | [![](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/mini-crescent-entrance.jpg)](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/crescent-entrance.jpg) | [![](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/mini-stonehive-staircase.jpg)](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/stonehive-staircase.jpg) | [![](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/mini-valdeholm-zek.jpg)](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/valdeholm-zek.jpg) | [![](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/mini-ashengate-scale.jpg)](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/scenery/ashengate-scale.jpg) |

### The Beginning

- The Serpent's Spine expansion was announced on July 11, 2006, and went live on September 19, 2006.
- The initial expansion announcement may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/history/news-2006jul11.html). The initial patch message may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/history/patches-2006-2.html).

### What's Going On

- After Venril Sathir's curse was released into the Nest and destroyed the dragon eggs, it was clear that the future of dragonkind was in danger.
- Six dragon children of the Nest — Venesh the Greenblood; Draton'ra, Master of the Void; Keikolin, Bringer of Enlightenment; Mysaphar, Seeker of All; Atathus the Red Lord; and Osh\`vir the Windspirit — got together and called themselves the Circle of the Crystalwing. They went against the wishes of their Elders and created a new race to protect against the growing threats. Through recruitment, coercion, and even kidnapping, the Circle created the Drakkin, a race of dragon-touched humans, and used the abandoned Ogre city of Nokk within the Serpent's Spine mountains to develop them.

### What's New

- A new playable race, the Drakkin
- Level increase to 75
- New /con system:

  - Grey - This creature is trivial to you and will give you no experience for killing it.
  - Green - This creature is not much of a threat, but will still give you some experience.
  - Light Blue - This creature is below your level, but high enough level to give you experience.
  - Dark Blue - This creature is below your level, but a solid challenge, giving more experience than a light blue mob.
  - White - This creature is the same level as you.
  - Yellow - This creature is slightly above your level.
  - Red - This creature is well above your level.

- "QUEST ITEM" tags on most quest items
- "Lore Information" tab on certain items
- New stat: "SV CORRUPTION" (some NPCs' AE attacks are corruption-based, requiring a corruption cure)
- New out-of-combat regeneration system allows you to sit down and rest after being out of combat for 30 seconds (group) or 5 minutes (raid). An icon displays your status:

  - Crossed Swords - You are currently in combat.
  - Hour Glass - You are recovering from combat and cannot rest yet. In addition to the hourglass, there is a timer bar beneath the endurance bar that will count down as you get closer to being rested.
  - Poison Drop - You are no longer in combat, but cannot rest because of a debuff that is affecting you. You must get rid of the debuff before you can rest. Certain debuffs now have a yellow border instead of a red one. These debuffs do not affect your ability to rest.
  - Void - The void is the default icon and indicates that you are not in-combat and you can rest at any time.
  - Energy Swirl - The energy swirl indicates that you are currently resting.

### Zones

- A list of zones in The Serpent's Spine may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zcont=The+Serpent%27s+Spine).
- A screenshot of the animated in-game map of the mountains of Serpent's Spine may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/gallery/ingamemaps/tss.jpg).

### Travel to The Serpent's Spine

- A portal stone to Crescent Reach in the neutral section of the Plane of Knowledge (just west of the Plane of Tranquility stone) will land you in a safe area of Blightfire Moors.
- The Blightfire Moors may also be reached through Highpass Hold.
- Wizards are able to port and translocate to Blightfire Moors, Sunderock Springs, and Icefall Glacier.
- Druids are able to port and translocate to Blightfire Moors, Direwind Cliffs, and The Steppes.
- One character per account recives [Scepter of Draconic Calling](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=51446) ("/claim 10"). Its effect is a self-port to Crescent Reach reuseable after 72 minutes.
- Goru'kar Mesa is a destination in the Guild Hall portal.

* * *

**Spells in The Serpent's Spine**

Most spells in this expansion are tiered. When you scribe a Rank II spell in your book, the Rank I spell will be overwritten; likewise for Rank III.

- **Rank I spells**are vendor-bought, and are NO TRADE:
  - **Bards:** [Raistedas Curlbraids](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22368) in Goru'kar Mesa (-665, +1025).
  - **Beastlords:** [Tavin of the Wild](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22690) in Icefall Glacier (-2105, -1135).
  - **Berserkers:** [Sensei Splitscale](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22687) in Sunderock Springs (-1430, +850).
  - **Clerics:** [Kalladrahn the Pure](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22686) in Sunderock Springs (-1390, +825).
  - **Druids:** [Rang Kelliam](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22691) in The Steppes (-340, +280).
  - **Enchanters:** [a bixie imposter](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22692) in Stone Hive (hedge maze; +585, -995).
  - **Magicians:** [Pesha of the Mist](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22684) in Sunderock Springs (+1615, -1065).
  - **Monks:** [Sensei Splitscale](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22687) in Sunderock Springs (-1430, +850).
  - **Necromancers:** [Outcast Sayrus](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22576) in Direwind (-1120, -515).
  - **Paladins:** [Inquisitor Harona](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22685) in Sunderock Springs (-1385, +795).
  - **Rangers:** [Hemmet, Master Sureshot](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22418) in The Steppes (-700, +1305).
  - **Rogues:** [Sensei Splitscale](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22687) in Sunderock Springs (-1430, +850).
  - **Shadowknights:** [Outcast Erralock](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22437) in Direwind (-1120, -515).
  - **Shamans:** [Ezna Connoy](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22363) in Icefall Glacier (-2105, -1135).
  - **Warriors:** [Sensei Splitscale](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22687) in Sunderock Springs (-1430, +850).
  - **Wizards:** [Jaelle Florica](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22689) in Goru'kar Mesa (-720, +1020).

- **Rank II spells** are dropped randomly throughout upper level zones (Ashengate, Frostcrypt, Direwind Cliffs, Valdeholm, Icefall Glacier, The Steppes and Sunderock), and are droppable.

- **Rank III spells** are obtained through raids, and are NO TRADE. Loot [Glowing Obsidian Runes](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=52101) from various raid encounters and hand then to [Kalim'Kar the Gold](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22817) in Ashengate through [this quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3629).

- The Magician **summon spells** are dropped by random mobs. They are tradeable.

- The Enchanter **Drakkin illusion** is obtained through [this quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3588) from [Assistant Avellone](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22564) in Blightfire Moors. The illusion spell is NO TRADE.

- Druid **portal spells** are bought from [Tienen the Wanderer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22571) in Direwind (-1255, -15), [Emelia Daeren](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22419) in The Steppes (+495, -1285), and [Gray Peacebloom](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22568) in Blightfire (+360, +1360).

- Wizard **portal spells** are bought from [Kulvinia Brightflame](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22567) in Blightfire (-145, +575).

- There are also various **bane spells/skills** for some classes. It is not known for sure what drops these ( **information about how to obtain them needed: email [Railus](mailto:railus@allakhazam.com) or [Fleven](mailto:fleven@allakhazam.com) if you wish to share**).

* * *

**Progression Through The Serpent's Spine** (Misc.)

Most areas in this expansion are open to everyone. In addition to the normal task system (where one task brings a reward), many tasks within the expansion are part of a series, with rewards coming after completing multiple tasks (the low level ["Love in the Air" series](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3654), for example).

Still, there are a few places and areas which require keys and flags:

- Travelling among the ledges of Blackfeather Roost requires completion of various tasks:
  - [Travel to the First Ledge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3606)
  - [Travel to the Harpy Cliffs](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3672)
  - [Travel to the Harpy Grove](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3632)
  - [Travel to the Harpy Mesa](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3696)
  - [Reach the Royal Throne](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3695)

- To enter the depths of the Vergalid Mines, you must complete [this short key quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3707).

* * *

**Ashengate Progression Line** ( [Access Quest Entry Here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=4157))

- In order to **participate** in an Ashengate raid, you must have ally faction with [Scholars of Solusek](http://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/faction.html?faction=489) and [Infiltrators and Traitors of Ashengate](http://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/faction.html?faction=494) (click those links for lists of tasks to do to raise faction) ... AND have received the character flag at the end of the [Vergalid's End](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3930) raid.

- In order to **request** Ashengate raids, you must have each of the above, AND the following:

  - [Captain Zheren](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23292)'s first four tasks (location -1220, +630 in Sunderock (the Drakkin camp)):

    - 1\. [Scouting Sunderock](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3646)
    - 2\. [Dyn\`Leth's Artillery](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3692)
    - 3\. [The Ashengate Orders](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3693)
    - 4\. [Dyn\`Leth's Mine](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3697)

  - A 24-player [Into the Leviathan's Lair](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3668) raid from [Sergeant Kazzar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23426) (standing next to Zheren).
  - A 54-player [Vergalid's End](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3930) raid, also from Sergeant Kazzar.

  - The task " [Severing the Strings](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3911)" from [Jenray, Envoy of Ro](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23952) in Direwind (tent just outside lava moat to the right of the Ashengate zone)...
    - _Note: In order to request this task, you must have completed Zheren's first four task and have participated in a successful [Into the Leviathan's Lair](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3668) 24-person raid._
    - _This task must be completed in conjunction with the_ [_Vergalid's End_](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3930) _raid._
  - The task " [The Pretender](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3900)" from [Zhubis the Griffin Tamer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23340) at +960, -1045 in Direwind (south of Gatekeeper Kor, on the other side of the valley).

    - _Note: In order to request this task, you must have completed Zheren's first four task and have participated in a successful [Into the Leviathan's Lair](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3668) 24-person raid._
    - _This task must be completed in conjunction with the_ [_Vergalid's End_](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3930) _raid._

* * *

**Frostcrypt Progression Line**

- In order to participate in a Frostcrypt raid, you will need to reach ally faction with [Wraithguard Leadership](http://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/faction.html?faction=491) and complete [Fergarin Bardon I](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23628)'s group and raid tasks. Each requires ally faction with Wraithguard Leadership to request and gives a positive faction hit to everyone involved:
  - [Intercepted Correspondence](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3931) (group)
  - [Lost Letter](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3932) (group)
  - [Confession](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3933) (group)
  - [Kill Kangur](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3934) (raid)
  - [Udengar the Traitor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3954) (raid)
  - [Speak with the King](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3955) (raid)
- Check the and [Wraithguard Leadership](http://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/faction.html?faction=491) faction listings to see all associated tasks and quests available through them.
- In order to request a Frostcrypt raid, you need all of the above AND ally faction with [Wraithguard](http://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/faction.html?faction=481) (different from "Wraithguard Leadership")
- You may now request the Frostcrypt raid from Fergarin (say "Frostcrypt" to him) and zone in by saying "Fergarin" to Wraithguard Sergeant Thavin.

* * *

**Quests to do While Progressing Through The Serpent's Spine**

- **Charm of Lore**
  - [This quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3666) involves the entire Serpent's Spine expansion, and involves collecting various artifacts from solo, group, and raid encounters in order to upgrade a charm received from [Librarian Hemfar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22639) of Crescent Reach.

- **Wanderlust Guild Charm**
  - Another quest involving the entire expansion is the Wanderlust Guild Charm series which begins with [this task](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3680).

- **Bypass the Ashengate Guards**
  - Unless you can feign death, fade, or SOS by them, the two [Guardians of the Gate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23345) in the Direwind Cliffs will probably cause you some annoyance. Complete three relatively quick tasks beginning with [this one](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3607) to bypass them.

- **Warriors, Kings, and Heroes**
  - Some very good augmentations are rewarded after completion of these tasks: They are the discs of [Warriors](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3611), [Kings](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3612), and [Heroes](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3613) given by [Rokiln the Hunter](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22855) in Vergalid.

- **Familiars for Everyone**
  - Priests and Pure Casters can summon a familiar using the Direwolf Totem of Spirit obtained from [this quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3610).
  - Hybrids and Pure Melees can summon a familiar using the Direwolf Totem of Battle obtained from [this quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3614).

- Many more tasks were added with the Serpent's Spine expansion. The list here is made up of the highlights. A complete list of TSS quests and tasks listed in the Allakhazam database can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/adv_qsearch.html?terms=&classes=All&races=All&szone=0&izone=0&minlevel=&level=&status=Any&era=serpents&type=0&goal=0&debug=1&act=Search%21) (this is a [premium](http://www.allakhazam.com/Premium_Chart.html)-only link).

* * *

**Armor Sets in The Serpent's Spine**

- **Kimem's Armor (Levels 20-30)**
  - [Kimem Saydel](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22681) in Crescent Reach offers armor quests for [chain](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3597), [leather](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3600), [plate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3598), and [silk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3603) classes These quests result in armor pieces which have in the neighborhood of 10-25hp/mana/end.

- **Scout Madu's Armor (Levels 40-45)**
  - [Scout Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=23055)in Goru\`kar Mesa offers armor quests involving Mesa mobs. These quests result in armor pieces which have in the neighborhood of 35-45hp/mana/end.
    - [Bear Pelts for Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3634) (Feet)
    - [Kill Fantoma](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3637) (Legs)
    - [Kill Ursalua](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3623) (Chest)
    - [More Supplies for Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3664) (Arms)
    - [Snake Skins for Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3635) (Hands)
    - [Spider Silk for Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3636) (Head)
    - [Wolf Skins for Madu](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3633) (Wrist)

- **Tenish's Armor (Single Groupable; Levels 71-75)**
  - [Tenish](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22650) in Crescent Reach offers armor quests for [chain](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3592), [leather](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3595), [plate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3590), and [silk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3594) classes. These quests result in armor pieces which have in the neighborhood of 210-240hp/mana/end.
  - Each piece may be upgraded to include stat bonuses as well ( [chain](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3593), [leather](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3596), [plate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3591), and [silk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3691) respectively).

- **Terraxs's Armor (Raid Required; Level 75)**

  - [Terraxs](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=22946) in Crescent Reach also offers armor quests for [chain](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3772), [leather](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3776), [plate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3774), and [silk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3778) classes. These quests result in armor pieces which have in the neighborhood of 450-475hp/mana/end.
  - Each piece may be upgraded to include stat bonuses as well ( [chain](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3773), [leather](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3777), [plate](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3775), and [silk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3779) respectively).

# Jokes on You

**Level:** 45 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 45
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

Assistant Avellone may be found at -210, -1690, in a group of quest NPCs in a swamp in the southeast portion of the zone.
You say, 'Hail, Assistant Avellone'
Assistant Avellone says, 'Do you have time to look into some [agricultural] technology for me?  If not, I have a few [other tasks] for Vreshnar that I was going to look into today.'
You say, 'What other tasks?'
Assistant Avellone says, 'I need to track down [Prunae] and look into some [pranksters].'
You say, 'Who is Prunae?'
Assistant Avellone says, 'Well I heard Vreshnar muttering about her. You see, she helped him put together the Bixie Illusion spell, but there seems to be something wrong.  She donned an illusion and barged into Stone Hive, and hasn't been seen since.  She seemed furious!  Then yesterday, Vreshnar was stunned by a fellow Wyrmkin.  It shouldn't have even been possible!  We must [find her].'
You say, 'I will find her.'
Assistant Avellone says, 'She may still be in disguise, but I just know something is wrong.  Perhaps she has been trapped.'
You have been assigned the task 'Jokes on You'.
Prunae has gone missing, and there are some doubts as to her loyalty to Crescent Reach.  Find her within Stone Hive and determine whether she has been imprisoned, or turned Bixie and is sharing secrets within the hive.
Reward(s):
You gain experience!!
Explore the slave hold of Stone Hive (0/1) - Stone Hive
Head to Stone Hive and into the large, main hive in the middle of the zone.  Go up the stairs in the center room, then through the doorway on the west side.  Turn north and head up the elevator by clicking on it. Then, go through the room on that floor, and you will eventually reach the slave hold to the east.
Find a clue as to who is telling the truth (0/1) - Stone Hive
There you will find either a slavemistress or Slavemistress Hizja. They are very weak and easily soloable by a level 70+ Enchanter with or without a pet.  Once you kill either one of them, they may drop an "Imposter's Clue".
Determine who the imposter is and kill them! (0/1) - Stone Hive
Once you loot this, the Caged Drakkin nearby will turn into a bixie and attack you.  This mob is again easily soloable by at 70.  Once you slay the Caged Drakkin, the quest is over and you will be able to choose your reward:
Option 1: Devious Drakkin Potion
MAGIC ITEM NO TRADE
EXPENDABLE Charges: 1
Potion Belt Enabled
Effect: Illusion: Drakkin of Osh`vir (Casting Time: 0.8)
Recast Delay: 5 seconds
WT: 0.2 Size: SMALL
Class: ALL
Race: ALL
Illusion: Drakkin of Osh`vir
Spell Effect: Cloaks you in a shimmering illusion that makes you appear to be a Drakkin.
-or-
Option 2: Devious Drakkin Disguise
MAGIC ITEM LORE ITEM NO TRADE
Level Needed: ENC(30)
Skill: Divination
Mana Cost: 10
Spell Effect: Cloaks you in a shimmering illusion that makes you appear to be a Drakkin.
WT: 1.2 Size: TINY
Race: ALL
The Enchanter spell scroll scribes into your spellbook as "Illusion: Drakkin of Draton`ra".Submitted by: Amnesiaa Dragonbane, Dragon Council, BertoxxulousRewards:
- Devious Drakkin Disguise [item=51830]
- Devious Drakkin Potion [item=51831]

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the outline for armor tasks offered by Tenish. They can be completed with a single group. These tasks result in armor with stats in the neighborhood of 215-240hp/mana/end. After completing one of these tasks, you can request a task in which you add stat bonuses to your armor piece. That outline can be found here [quest=3591]._
_Find Tenesh in Crescent Reach at location -1400, -1440, -55 (up a ramp west of the "find-able" Librarian Hemfar)._
You say, 'Hail, Tenish'
Tenish says 'Vasha, Friend! Excuse me if I seem distracted but I was just studying this text on ancient and powerful [artifacts] that Librarian Hemfar has compiled.'
You say, 'Powerful artifacts?'
Tenish says 'Yes indeed! This entire area is rich with ancient and forgotten magic just waiting to be discovered and harnessed. I believe I have learned how to tap into some of the most powerful traces of magic lingering in this area and use it to imbue ordinary [armor] items with extraordanary properties!'
You say, 'What armor?'
Tenish says 'Oh, armor of all types! Anything from Platemail to Silk Robes, I believe I have discovered the secret to creating some of the most powerful protective items ever seen on Norrath. The only hurdle I face is I haven't been able to locate any of the [components] that this tome hints at.'
You say, 'What components?'
Tenish says 'Here, I have prepared a list. If you would like to help me search for the items on this list I would be happy to share the resulting items with you. I assure you, \_\_\_\_\_\_, You won't be sorry if you assist me in locating these items!'
_The following tasks are available, depending on your class (the name of the task is the piece of armor you will receive):_
Bards

Marred Plate Boots
Marred Plate Gloves

Marred Plate Vambraces
Marred Plate Greaves
Marred Plate Breastplate
_You have been assigned the task '\_\_\_\_\_'._
Tenish believes that he can help you craft new and powerful armors. All you need to do is collect several components and return them to Tenish so he can construct the final item. Tenish believes that you should focus your search far to the North.
From what Tenish can gather there are two types of ancient artifacts that he thinks he can use to construct your new armor items.
Ancient artifacts have become infused with a magical coating that looks strangely like Frost. Search along the path to the Giant city we have heard rumors of for such artifacts.
The ash from a distant volcano has fused itself with other artifacts, lending strange properties to them. Search along the path to this volcano for these artifacts.
The molds you will need can be found in the same areas as the artifacts although they may be slightly harder to locate. The local denizens of these areas have become aware of the unique properties of these molds and the most powerful among them may be hoarding them for their own purposes.
_For the Chest Piece:_
4x Frosted Scrap Metal
An Ancient Chestplate Mold
_For the Legs Piece:_
4x Ash-Covered Scrap Metal
An Ancient Legplate Mold
_For the Arms Piece:_
3x Frosted Scrap Metal
An Ancient Armplate Mold
_For the Hands Piece:_
2x Ash-Covered Scrap Metal
An Ancient Plate Glove Mold
_For the Head Piece:_
3 x Ash-Covered Scrap Metal
An Ancient Helm Mold
_For the Boots Piece:_
2x Frosted Scrap Metal
An Ancient Plate Boot Mold
_For the Wrist Piece:_
1x Ash-Covered Scrap Metal
An Ancient Plate Bracer Mold
_Frosted Scrap Metal drops from mobs in Icefall Glacier and The Steppes._
_Ash-Covered Scrap Metal drops from mobs in Direwind Cliffs and Sunderock Springs._
_The molds drop from various 75+ named._
_Give the required items to Tenash in exchange for your armor piece and completion of the task._
Tenish says 'My theory was correct! This is fantastic! Now, friend, I must ask if you are prepared to go [further] with this experiment and see if we cannot, together, create something truly fantastic?'
_This is the lead-in to another series of tasks, in which you can enhance you armor with stat bonuses._
**Submitted by:** Laurelina/Menelin, The Silent Minority, Prexus

Tenish believes that he can further increase the power of the item you previously created by adding in a powersource, a collector of magical energies. Tenish has been able to confirm that such items have been discovered in both the Vergalid Mines and the Giant City of Valdeholm.
Bring back the item you created and a powersource and deliver it to Tenish so that he can complete the final stage of your experiment.
Quest Steps:
1\. Deliver 1 [Part 1 Armor Piece] to Tenish
2\. Deliver 1 A Softly Glowing Powersource to Tenish
_A Softly Glowing Powersource drops from various named mobs in Valdeholm and Vergalid Mines._
_Hand in these two items to receive your upgraded armor, stat bonuses inclued._

Bring back the item you created and a powersource and deliver it to Tenish so that he can complete the final stage of your experiment.
Quest Steps:
1\. Deliver 1 [Part 1 Armor Piece] to Tenish
2\. Deliver 1 A Faintly Powersource to Tenish
_A Faintly Powersource drops from various named mobs in Valdeholm and Vergalid Mines._
_Hand in these two items to receive your upgraded armor, stat bonuses inclued._

Entered: Fri Sep 22 17:02:35 2006
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the outline for armor tasks offered by Tenish. They can be completed with a single group. These tasks result in armor with stats in the neighborhood of 215-240hp/mana/end. After completing one of these tasks, you can request a task in which you add stat bonuses to your armor piece. That outline can be found here [quest=3691]._
_Find Tenesh in Crescent Reach at location -1400, -1440, -55 (up a ramp west of the "find-able" Librarian Hemfar)._
You say, 'Hail, Tenish'
Tenish says 'Vasha, Friend! Excuse me if I seem distracted but I was just studying this text on ancient and powerful [artifacts] that Librarian Hemfar has compiled.'
You say, 'Powerful artifacts?'
Tenish says 'Yes indeed! This entire area is rich with ancient and forgotten magic just waiting to be discovered and harnessed. I believe I have learned how to tap into some of the most powerful traces of magic lingering in this area and use it to imbue ordinary [armor] items with extraordanary properties!'
You say, 'What armor?'
Tenish says 'Oh, armor of all types! Anything from Platemail to Silk Robes, I believe I have discovered the secret to creating some of the most powerful protective items ever seen on Norrath. The only hurdle I face is I haven't been able to locate any of the [components] that this tome hints at.'
You say, 'What components?'
Tenish says 'Here, I have prepared a list. If you would like to help me search for the items on this list I would be happy to share the resulting items with you. I assure you, \_\_\_\_\_\_, You won't be sorry if you assist me in locating these items!'
_The following tasks are available, depending on your class (the name of the task is the piece of armor you will receive):_
Enchanters
Attuned Silk Cap
Attuned Silk Gloves
Attuned Silk Pants
Attuned Silk Robe
Attuned Silk Sandals
Attuned Silk Sleeves
Attuned Silk Wristband
Magicians
Shrouded Silk Cap
Shrouded Silk Gloves
Shrouded Silk Pants
Shrouded Silk Robe
Shrouded Silk Sandals
Shrouded Silk Sleeves
Shrouded Silk Wristband
Necromancers
Vexed Silk Cap
Vexed Silk Gloves
Vexed Silk Pants
Vexed Silk Robe
Vexed Silk Sandals
Vexed Silk Sleeves
Vexed Silk Wristband
Wizards
Keeper Silk Cap
Keeper Silk Gloves
Keeper Silk Pants
Keeper Silk Robe
Keeper Silk Sandals
Keeper Silk Sleeves
Keeper Silk Wristband
_You have been assigned the task '\_\_\_\_\_'._
Tenish believes that he can help you craft new and powerful armors. All you need to do is collect several components and return them to Tenish so he can construct the final item. Tenish believes that you should focus your search far to the North.
From what Tenish can gather there are two types of ancient artifacts that he thinks he can use to construct your new armor items.
Ancient artifacts have become infused with a magical coating that looks strangely like Frost. Search along the path to the Giant city we have heard rumors of for such artifacts.
The ash from a distant volcano has fused itself with other artifacts, lending strange properties to them. Search along the path to this volcano for these artifacts.
The molds you will need can be found in the same areas as the artifacts although they may be slightly harder to locate. The local denizens of these areas have become aware of the unique properties of these molds and the most powerful among them may be hoarding them for their own purposes.
_For the Chest Piece:_
4x Frosted Swatch
An Ancient Robe Pattern
_For the Legs Piece:_
4x Ash-Covered Swatch
An Ancient Silk Leggings Pattern
_For the Arms Piece:_
3x Frosted Swatch
An Ancient Silk Armwrap Pattern
_For the Hands Piece:_
2x Ash-Covered Swatch
An Ancient Silk Glove Pattern
_For the Head Piece:_
3x Ash-Covered Swatch
An Ancient Silk Skullcap Pattern
_For the Boots Piece:_
2x Frosted Swatch
An Ancient Silk Boot Pattern
_For the Wrist Piece:_
1x Ash-Covered Swatch
An Ancient Silk Bracer Pattern
_Frosted Swatches drop from mobs in Icefall Glacier and The Steppes._
_Ash-Covered Swatches drop from mobs in Direwind Cliffs and Sunderock Springs._
_The molds drop from various 75+ named._
_Give the required items to Tenash in exchange for your armor piece and completion of the task._
Tenish says 'My theory was correct! This is fantastic! Now, friend, I must ask if you are prepared to go [further] with this experiment and see if we cannot, together, create something truly fantastic?'
_This is the lead-in to another series of tasks, in which you can enhance you armor with stat bonuses._
**Submitted by:** Laurelina/Menelin, The Silent Minority, Prexus

This is the outline for armor tasks offered by Kimem Saydel in Crescent Reach. They can be completed with a single group (even solo if you are of level). These tasks result in armor with stats in the neighborhood of 20-30hp/mana/end. Armor pieces have a required level of 20.

Recently, I've seen adventurers returning from the Blightfire Moors and bringing with them [materials] that have picked up a bit of the curse that plagues that land. I've had a chance to study some of it and I believe I can tap into the magic within the [materials] to fashion armor that would protect you on your adventures.'
You say, 'What materials?'
Kimem Saydel says 'The materials I need depend upon the piece of armor you want fashioned.'

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _There are six islands in Blackfeather Roost, each with its own requirements for access (you can port from island to island after completing a short task for each):_
\- The Base (entered from Goru'kar Mesa)
\- The First Ledge (complete this task)
\- The Harpy Grove ( see this task [quest=3632])
\- The Harpy Cliffs ( see this task [quest=3672])

---

_This task begins with Fenith who is located at the opposite end of the island upon zoning into Blackfeather Roost (near the tame griffon)._
You say, 'Hail, Fenith'
Fenith says 'What? Who's there? Oh my [nerves]!'
You say, 'Your nerves?'
Fenith says 'Ever since my capture I just can't seem to stop shaking. I'm so afraid all the time now, especially of the [griffons]. Of course, that's the worst part because I have to feed them every day!'
You say, 'What griffons?'
Fenith says 'Oh those hateful fearsome beasts! They haunted my nightmares as a young satyr and now all my nightmares have come true. A horrible harpy, Ashrana was her name, swooped down from above commanding a griffon and whisked me, bound and helpless, [into the air]!'
You say, 'Into the air!'
Fenith says 'Why would you even ask me about riding them? You can't possibly want to ride a griffon [do] you?'
You say, 'I do'
Fenith says 'If you do then you're crazier than you look. Fine, I'll help you, but only if you help me! You can feed the griffon today instead of me! Haha! You see, if you want to ride the griffon, first you have to feed it or it just might eat you instead!. Combine some bear meat with a little snake meat, add in a puma paw and a tuft of bear fur and put it all in this Griffon Treat Kit and feed it to the griffon in the nest over there. If you're crazy enough to get that close to such a dangerous beast, he might let you ride him from place to place. Or he might eat your hand. One or the other . . .'
You have been assigned the task 'Blackfeather Roost, The Ledge'.

---

If you want to ride the griffon first you have to feed it. Combine some bear meat with a little snake meat, add in a puma paw and a tuft of bear fur and put it all in this Griffon Treat Kit and feed it to the griffon in the nest over there. If you're crazy enough to get that close to such a dangerous beast he might let you ride him from place to place. Or he might eat your hand, one or the other...
_You receive Fenith's Griffon Treat Kit, a 4 slot container._

_The treat is made by combining the 4 items in the Treat Kit. All can be hunted in the area close to Fenith. Give it to "a tame griffon"._

9. Return to Fenith to learn more about the Griffons 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)



You say, 'Hail, Fenith'




Fenith says 'So you managed to feed the griffon and keep all your fingers?! Huh! Well, don't try feeding that same stale treat to the next griffon you encounter -- it won't work. Each griffon likes a different treat and it's up to you to find it. If you like, I can give you a [hint] of what the next griffon has a taste for.'
_This is the lead-in to the second task, "Blackfeather Roost, The Grove"._

# Zryan #1: The Gray Legion

**Level:** 70 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 70
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This is the first of five tasks in a series. (At one point in this series you receive the Ashengate Seal, an item which allows you to bypass the golems guarding the gate to Ashengate.)
This task begins in the Direwind Cliffs with Scout Zryan, located just east of Sunderock Springs zoneline by a rock with another NPC, at -1620, -1010.  He is the one crouching.
You say, 'Hail, Scout Zryan'
Scout Zryan says 'Greetings, ______!  It's nice to find a friendly face in these forsaken lands.  I've been scouting the Ashengate temple, looking for a way in.  Dyn`leth's armies keep the [approach] heavily guarded, but we may just have a chance.'
You say, 'What approach?'
Scout Zryan says 'When outnumbered, the superior warrior stalks with patience and strikes only when the time is right.  Here is what must be done. . . '
You have been assigned the task 'The Gray Legion'.
Kill 10 Gray Legion Soldiers - Direwind Cliffs
Kill 2 Gray Legion Clerics - Direwind Cliffs
Kill 2 Gray Legion Mages - Direwind Cliffs
Kill 5 Gray Legion Sergeants - Direwind Cliffs
This last step becomes available after completion of the previous steps.
Deliver Orzhok's Shattered Shield to Scout Zryan - Direwind Cliffs
Your task 'The Gray Legion' has been updated.
Zryan says, 'Well done.  You are now ready for the main attack on the [approach].'
This is the lead-in to the next task, "The Approach".
Your faction standing with Crusade of the Scale got better.
Your faction standing with Forces of Dyn'leth got worse.
Your faction standing with Circle of the Crystalwing got better.
You gain experience!!
These mobs that you need to kill can be found just south-east of the Scout's location. They are in groups of usually 2 soldiers, 2-3 orcs (level 65) and either a cleric or mage. The sergeants roam from group to group and can see-invis. It is suggested to pull to a spot near the task giver. These mobs have a fairly large assist range, so FD pulling and/or crowd control is a must.
Orzhok's Shattered can be looted at anytime (looting is not part of the task just turning it in is).
Orzhok Shattered Shield is dropped by Captain Orzhok (drops 6), who is 75+ and rampages.Submitted by: Restorer, Quellious ServerRewards:

Valdeholm zone. Invised, you can run around and trigger the first 2 parts.
The wolves don't summon; nor do the wolf tenders. Kill them and return to
Malgen for your reward which is a familiar that looks like a shaman pet.

The loss of these wolves will put a damper on the orcish raids. The Kobold
livestock should be much safer with the dismantling of the pack.

This solo task does not have a time limit.
This task begins with Rokiln the Hunter in Vergalid Mines.
You say, 'Hail, Rokiln the Hunter'
The spirit speaks slowly, 'Greeetinnngs. . . Though you be not my kindred, you have proven your worth by delivering these noble hunting trophies to honor my grave.  In return for this great service, Troll, I will tell you the [tale] of this tomb.'
You say, 'What tale?'
Rokiln the Hunter says 'For many years, my ancestors held these mines, until the dragon men came. . .'
Rokiln now offer his four different tasks...
Rokiln's tomb was once a vestibule for storing artifacts of the giants. The tomb has since been raided and looted by drakkin who have overrun the mines. Among the objects stolen by Dyn'leth's armies was an old giant artifact known as the Shield of the Otherworld. The artifact held special spiritual significance to the giants as it contained the names of all their fallen warriors, heroes, and kings on three interlocking disks. Dyn'leth, realizing this significance, smashed the disk and awarded the broken shards to his various captains and commanders as trophies.
The Disk of the Warriors remained in the Vergalid Mines with the followers of Dyn'Leth assigned there.
Return the remnants of the Disk of the Warriors to Rokiln at his tomb.
Return the First Fragment of the Warrior's Disk to Rokiln's Tomb 0/1 (Vergalid Mines)

Return the Fifth Fragment of the Warrior's Disk to Rokiln's Tomb 0/1 (Vergalid Mines)
Return the Sixth Fragment of the Warrior's Disk to Rokiln's Tomb 0/1 (Vergalid Mines)

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _Find Pack Leader Orgak in the orc fort (located in the northeastern corner of the zone)._
_Unless you have sufficient faction from killing kobolds, you will have to clear the fort to safely talk to him. He is KOS, but won't attack. The kobolds (that you have to kill to raise your orc faction) in the NW caves of the zone are in the same caves as the cages and pups you will have to feed. Might make a label on your map._
You say, 'Hail, Pack Leader Orgak'
Pack Leader Orgak says 'What! Why you here? What you want with master of [wolves]?'
You say, 'Wolves?'
Pack Leader Orgak says 'Wolves! My wolves! Orgak the packer leader, Orgak [take care] of the wolves.'
You say, 'Take care?'
Pack Leader Orgak says 'Not Orgak's fault! Kobolds take wolves when Orgak out behind tree! Stupid kobolds eat wolves. You! You [go get] wolves for Orgak!'
You say, 'I go get'
_You have been assigned the task 'Free the Wolves'._
Pack Leader Orgak says 'Yes! Yes! Go now. But you need [meat] for wolf! Give meat to wolf. Wolf then know you friend!'
You say, 'What meat?'
Pack Leader Orgak says 'Yes! Yes! Here is meat!'
_You receive 3 x Meat for Orgak's Wolves. You need to travel to the far west side of the zone, and enter the western kobold caves. In the northern rooms are three cages with one wolf in each cage. Break open the cage, and hand each wolf a piece of meat. If there are no cages there, the task should update anyways._
Cages must be open in order to feed the meat to the wolves.
_Once the cage is open:_
a captured dire wolf pup sniffs at the meat for a moment, then wolfs it down. It immediately looks to you for more. Realizing suddenly that it is free, the wolf bolts away at blinding speed.
After all 3 wolves are freed, you need to kill 10 Nightmoon kobolds. When finished, head back to Orgak.
You say, 'Hail, Pack Leader Orgak'
The kobolds will think twice before stealing from Orgak again. With the return of his missing wolves, the pack should be back to full strength again.
_Reward for the quest:_
\- Direwolf Totem of Battle
- Direwolf Totem of Battle [item=51938]

Modified: Thu Aug 7 13:32:11 2025 | | Scout Madu [npc=23055] may be found at -1955, +540 just west of the Blightfire Moors zoneline. He's on Find (Ctrl+F).
You say, 'Hail, Scout Madu'
Scout Madu says 'Well met traveler. I have been asked to keep an eye on this area and to attempt to reduce any threats to Crescent Reach that might arise. I can always use someone that has the will to attempt dangerous scouting missions, if you're able to deal with the [unusual folk] that live in these parts.'
You say, 'What unusual folk?'
Scout Madu says 'It was the oddest thing. This fellow I don't know, one with the legs of a deer and the face of a human, stumbled into me the other day. And I mean stumbled literally. It seems that he had been very deep in his cups. He kept rambling on about his brother betraying him, but I never could get more out of him before he wandered off. How odd of him. Sorry, I do not know why you made me think of him, you obviously have your [wits] about you.'
You say, 'I have my wits about me.'
Scout Madu says 'Yes. Yes you do. If you would like to explore the area, I'm certain that Vulu over there has some work for you. But if you're looking for something more interesting than just wandering around, I might have some [tasks] that are a bit more dangerous that you could undertake for me.'
You say, 'What tasks?'
Scout Madu says 'I have many tasks available, but most of them require that you have much experience. When you think you are ready, come back to me and ask me about my more [difficult] tasks. In the meantime I have some tasks for ones with less experience, and I will even reward ones who help me with some [extra armor].'
You say, 'What extra armor?'
Scout Madu says 'I have extra armor that has been given to me for scouts of this region. If you wish, I can share some of this armor with you. I have armor that will cover your [forearms], [feet], [hands], [head], [arms], [legs], or [torso]. Which would you like to get? I will only give you one piece to fit each place on your body as we do not have an infinite amount of supplies, even with the supplies you will be getting me.'
You say, 'Torso.'
Scout Madu says 'The bears in this region are particularly territorial. There is one in particular that is above the rest. He has been named Ursalua by those that reside in this area. He will be difficult to find, and may only come out in response to you killing his brethren. I am confident that you are up to this task.'
_You have been assigned the task 'Kill Ursalua'._
1\. Slay Ursalua, the dreaded bear - Goru`kar Mesa

# Serpent's Spine Rank III Spells

**Level:** 71 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 71
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

Find Kalim'Kar the Gold, a drakkin NPC just to the right of the door into the fortress.
You say, 'Hail, Kalim'Kar the Gold'
Kalim'Kar the Gold says 'Shhh... Speak in hushed tones here, Gnome, lest we both be discovered. Did Keikolin [send] you as well?
You say, 'Keikolin.. send me?'
Kalim'Kar the Gold says 'Regardless, this is a dead end. I was charged with spreading the influence of magic by the great gold dragon herself. I've done all that I can, and can continue no [further].'
You say, 'Why can't you continue further?'
Kalim'Kar the Gold says 'To venture into Ashengate is suicide, and though I would gladly sacrifice myself to further Keikolin's cause, dying would accomplish nothing and destroy all the [work] I have accomplished thus far.'
You say, 'What work is that?'
Kalim'Kar the Gold says 'I can concentrate and improve existing magics, but not without possessing more fragments of arcane knowledge in the form of Glowing Obsidian Runes. They are locked away inside the Reliquary of Ashengate, and I have sensed them in other [places].'
You say 'What other places?'
Kalim'Kar the Gold says 'Frostcrypt, the mines of Vergalid, the giant stronghold called Valdeholm, and possibly elsewhere. Seek out the Glowing Obsidian Runes and return them to me, and I should be able to craft magic that you can utilize.'
From certain raid events in The Serpent's Spine, loot a Glowing Obsidian Rune. Bring the rune to Kalim`Kar in exchange for a Rank III spell.
Kalim'Kar the Gold is ecstatic, 'Yes, this is precisely what I required. As Keikolin commands, I bestow upon you a greater magic. Use it wisely.'

Entered: Sun Oct 1 06:03:01 2006
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _There are six islands in Blackfeather Roost, each with its own requirements for access (you can port from island to island after completing a short task for each):_
\- The Base (entered from Goru'kar Mesa)
\- The First Ledge ( see this task [quest=3606])
\- The Harpy Grove (complete this task)
\- The Harpy Cliffs ( see this task [quest=3672])
\- The Harpy Mesa ( see this task [quest=3696])
\- The Royal Throne ( see this task [quest=3695])

---

Do not bother trying anything if one does not have the quest in the quest window.
\-\-\- Having just the container does not count.
\-\-\- Having just the "treat" does not count.
When one has the quest AND the treat (sandwich), do give it to tame griffon on the second island.
_At the end of "Blackfeather Roost, The Ledge":_
Fenith says 'So you managed to feed the griffon and keep all your fingers?! Huh! Well, don't try feeding that same stale treat to the next griffon you encounter -- it won't work. Each griffon likes a different treat and it's up to you to find it. If you like, I can give you a [hint] of what the next griffon has a taste for.'
You say, 'Give me a hint'
Fenith says 'If you want to try your luck a second time you can feed the griffon on the next ledge over. Like I said each griffon likes a different type of treat so for him you'll need to combine bear meat and snake meat along with some spider eggs and snake sheddings in this Griffon Treat Kit if you want to win his friendship. Good luck to you, \_\_\_\_, but I still think you're crazy as a loon.'
_You receive Fenith's Griffon Treat Kit, a 4 slot container._
Give the treat to the Griffon on the first ledge 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
_The treat is made by combining the 4 items in the Treat Kit..._
Snake Meat
Bear Meat
Spider Eggs
Snake Sheddings
_All can be hunted/foraged in the area close to Fenith (spiders spawn inside the bear caves - they are aggro with the bears, so you may have to clear the area of bears first). Give it to "a tame griffon" on the 1st ledge. You reach this ledge by clicking on the nest of the griffon close to Fenith._
_The reward is experience and the ability to use the 2nd griffon nest in order to reach the harpy grove._
**Submitted by:** Lias Roxx, Defiant, Zek

Entered: Sun Oct 1 18:28:05 2006
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _Scout Madu may be found at -1955, +530._
You say, 'Hail, Scout Madu'
Scout Madu says 'Well met traveler. I have been asked to keep an eye on this area and to attempt to reduce any threats to Crescent Reach that might arise. I can always use someone that has the will to attempt dangerous scouting missions, if you're able to deal with the [unusual folk] that live in these parts.'
You say, 'What unusual folk?'
Scout Madu says 'It was the oddest thing. This fellow I don't know, one with the legs of a deer and the face of a human, stumbled into me the other day. And I mean stumbled literally. Entered: Mon Oct 2 01:56:42 2006
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is the first in a series of five quests. Completing the first four will flag you so that you may request raids with Sergeant Kazzar._
_Captain Zheren may be found at -1225, +620._
You say, 'Hail, Captain Zheren'
Captain Zheren says '\_\_\_\_\_\_! Aren't you a sight for sore eyes! We set up this camp a few weeks ago to scout the lands controlled by Dyn`leth. We expected [opposition], but nothing like this.'
You say, 'What opposition?'
Captain Zheren says 'Dyn`leth's armies control almost every piece of land between the mines to the east and the temple to the north. We are vastly outnumbered. Any strength you can lend to our [cause] would be much appreciated.'
You say, 'What cause?'
Captain Zheren says 'We are the Crusade of the Scale! We will not rest until Dyn`leth is dead and the Scale of Veeshan has been reclaimed!'
You have been assigned the task 'Scouting Sunderock'.
The Brotherhood of the Scale is busy fortifying their encampment. They need you to scout the rest of Sunderrock Valley while they prepare their defences. Explore the following areas and report back to Captain Zheran when you are succesful.
The Sulfur Bog
The Sulfur Bog to the northwest is a festering swamp created by the oozes. They leech nutrients from the earth and expel sulfur as a byproduct of their anatomy.
The Pass to Direwind
A foul wind blows from the Direwind cliffs to the north. Get close, but not too close.
Updates at +3000, -150, +350.
The Vergalid Slave Quarry
Many slaves keep Dyn`leth's mining operation running in the Vergalid Mines. You will find the quarry near the entrance to the mines.
Fiddleback's Hunting Grounds
You will recognize Fiddleback's vally by the strewn bodies of her drained victims. Tread lightly.
Starshine Springs
Starshine is a calm rock glade to the southeast where the faydrake Anaglass lives. You will find solace there.
Sunderock Geyser
A great plume arises from the valley floor. Beware of Doomfount, the water elemental that originally created the waterspout.
The Chimera Caves
The chimeras are the largest remaining pack of predators in the valley. Their den lies deep in a cave to the southwest.
Basilisk Valley
The basilisks of Sunderock gather in a great valley to the southeast where they breed and die. Be careful. It is their mating season and the basilisks of that great valley are sure to be more aggresive at this time of year.
_Explore the following areas:_
_The Sulfur Bog 2483.74, 1212.63 near Balloondabloop spawn_
_The Pass to Direwind_
_Vergalid Mines -912.38, -1549.83, 215_
_Fiddleback's Hunting Ground 1043, -865.60, 375.58_
_Starshine Hotsprings -2299.99, -602.58, 208 near Anaglass_
_Sunderock Geyser -461.86, 910.14, 201.45_
_The Chimera Caves -2427.96, 2009.22, 21.15 in caves towards Trideath_
_Basilisk Valley -2694.07, -1520.36, 15.79 towards Komodokin_
You say `Hail, Captain Zheren'

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This task is the first in a series of four. Completing the series opens the quest paths of three NPCs: Vunder the Dark, Akins, and Council Aide Shay._
_Find Guard Captain Minka, located in the back section of Scaleguard Barracks on the second level._
You say, 'Hail, Guard Captain Minka'
Guard Captain Minka says 'Alas, my mood is melancholy friend. Speak your business so I may continue to [wallow].'
You say, 'Wallow?'
Guard Captain Minka says 'Tis a long story, but I suppose airing my quandary might aid me. My heart has been besieged by a beautiful drakkin. Her name is Kamilah and the image of her in my mind drives me to great distraction. I'm meant to be the guard captain of this great city and I find I'm getting distracted. And while I would cut down any enemy without any hesitation I can't find the courage to tell her how I feel! Hm, you seem fairly brazen, perhaps you might [help] me.'
You say, 'I will help you'
_You have been assigned the task 'Love in the Air: Guard's Fancy'._
Guard Captain Minka says 'Kamilah is a jeweler in Artisan's Row on the second level of Crescent Reach. You will no doubt recognize her beauty very easily. I've been saving this gift for her until I had the courage to hand it to her. Take this to her and tell her it is from me! Oh how my spirits have been lifted!'
_Receive Wild Crescent Rose, and take it to Jeweler Kamilah in The Jeweled Serpent in Artisan's Row on the second level. Doing the hand-in completes the task._
_Your task 'Love in the Air: Guard's Fancy' has been updated._
Sadly, Kamilah cannot return affection to Guard Captain Minkah, but she does have a sparkle in her eye when she talks about Herald Nexeu.
You gain experience!!
Jeweler Kamilah says 'Oh, a gift?' Kamilah reads the card and the sparkle in her eye is extinguished and she starts to sneeze. 'Get this potpourri away from me! I'm allergic to those dried flowers! It figures. I've seen that guard on his patrol constantly eyeing me. More like leering. It makes my stomach turn. Now he's either trying to buy me or kill me? Why must the wrong [drakkin] always court me?'
_This is the lead-in to the next task in the series._

You say, 'Hail, Scout Madu'
Scout Madu says 'Well met traveler. I have been asked to keep an eye on this area and to attempt to reduce any threats to Crescent Reach that might arise. I can always use someone that has the will to attempt dangerous scouting missions, if you're able to deal with the [unusual folk] that live in these parts.'
You say, 'What unusual folk?'
Scout Madu says 'It was the oddest thing. This fellow I don't know, one with the legs of a deer and the face of a human, stumbled into me the other day. And I mean stumbled literally. It seems that he had been very deep in his cups. He kept rambling on about his brother betraying him, but I never could get more out of him before he wandered off. How odd of him. Sorry, I do not know why you made me think of him, you obviously have your [wits] about you.'
You say, 'I have my wits about me.'
Scout Madu says 'Yes. Yes you do. If you would like to explore the area, I'm certain that Vulu over there has some work for you. But if you're looking for something more interesting than just wandering around, I might have some [tasks] that are a bit more dangerous that you could undertake for me.'
You say, 'What tasks?'
Scout Madu says 'I have many tasks available, but most of them require that you have much experience. When you think you are ready, come back to me and ask me about my more [difficult] tasks. In the meantime I have some tasks for ones with less experience, and I will even reward ones who help me with some [extra armor].'
You say, 'What extra armor?'
Scout Madu says 'I have extra armor that has been given to me for scouts of this region. If you wish, I can share some of this armor with you. I have armor that will cover your [forearms], [feet], [hands], [head], [arms], [legs], or [torso]. Which would you like to get? I will only give you one piece to fit each place on your body as we do not have an infinite amount of supplies, even with the supplies you will be getting me.'
You say, 'Arms.'
Scout Madu says 'We have gotten low on certain supplies. Can you go out and gather some for me?'

Modified: Sat Sep 7 01:26:11 2024 | | Find Librarian Hemfar on the first floor of Crescent Reach.
You say, 'Hail, Librarian Hemfar'
Librarian Hemfar says 'Vasha, friend. I am the second-born of Keikolin the Enlightened, the most knowledgeable dragon of the Six. I am to aid in the education of drakkin and all who choose to enlighten their minds. I am a collector of artifacts and one of the founders of the Written Word and the Wanderlust Guild so we may learn from books and life. I have a unique gift that you may partake in if you [aid] in my work. Or you may simply ask me what [lore] I have gathered from artifacts in these lands.
You say, 'Aid your work?'
Librarian Hemfar says 'Yes, indeed. I have been given a sight-gift from Lady Keikolin. In many artifacts I can see within them and through the eyes of those they belonged to. It allows me to create a history that will become part of this library. Should you find any artifacts in the surrounding lands, bring them to me and I shall reward you and be able to add to the [volumes] of our great library.'
You say, 'What volumes?'
Librarian Hemfar says 'There are many creatures present and past that lived in these mountains, including the ogres that once lived in this very city. What exactly happened to them and their kind is somewhat of a mystery, but given time and the right artifacts, we shall learn more. I can reward you if you bring artifacts to me. Then, you may ask what [lore] and legend is available as people bring me artifacts and I can share the history with you when it is scribed.'

---

This is an ongoing quest to upgrade the charm, Serpent Seeker's Charm of Lore. After handing in at least five of the required items, you "get an option that looks like it's the same old exp, but click the option and it shows charm as reward".
As you hand in each of the following items from around the Serpent's Spine, the charm will increase in strength. It is not known which items boost stats, or how many are required for each increase. There does not appear to be any particular requirement for order, either. These are the items known to be involved. There are probably many more.
Items used in this quest read "This artifact might interest someone in Crescent Reach" when the spell Identify is cast upon them.
Ashengate

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This 24 person (max) raid task begins with Sergeant Kazzar in Sunderock Springs. You must have at least 6 people in your group in order to request this task, they all don't have to be in the zone and can even be offline._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task are tasks 1-4 of Captain Zheren's task arc. There are no pre-requisites for participating in the task._
_Sergeant Kazzar stands next to the Captain._
You say, 'Hail, Sergeant Kazzar'
Sergeant Kazzar says 'Our Crusade struggles to hold a basecamp here on this mesa. The fight has not been easy. Our enemies still plot and wait within the [Vergalid mines] to the east.'
You say, 'Vergalid Mines?'
Sergeant Kazzar says 'You have fought long and hard against our enemies, \_\_\_\_. You are almost ready to lead a strike force against the Dragon Vergalid. First, you must take care of one of Vergalid's allies within the mines. Are you [willing] to do this?'
You say, 'I am willing'

You have been assigned the task 'Vergalid Mines: Into the Leviathan's Lair'
The direction to the entry to your instanced zone(s) have been marked on your compass. It leads to a door in the rocks to the northeast behind Fiddleback at +1600, -1500.
Venture into the Vergalid Mines and locate Kellak the Serpent Lord. Rain death down on the serpent. Do not return until the deed is done.
Enter the Vergalid Mines 0/1 (Sunderock Springs)
_The zone-in to the instance is at loc 1610, -1490, 390._
Slay Kellak the Serpent Lord 0/1 (Vergalid Mines)
_Trash mobs in this zone mostly see invis, so you'll have to kill your way to Kellak's lair._
_Once engaged, Kellak the Serpent Lord seems to be rooted in place. He sits in the water but you have to damage him above the water so levitate will be needed. He spawns three adds called "Kellak`s offspring" which proc "Serpent's Venom":_
Serpent's Venom: Single Target, Cold (-150)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 500
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 500 per tick
3: Increase Poison Counter by 9
4: Increase Poison Counter by 9
5: Increase Poison Counter by 9
_These adds are rootable. Best option is to offtank or root adds away from the raid while everyone burns down Kellak. Kellak himself hits for a max ~8,000 and casts "Electrify" and "Serpent's Venom":_
Electrify: Unknown(32) 300', Magic (-400)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 6000
Serpent's Venom: Single Target, Cold (-150)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 500
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 500 per tick
3: Increase Poison Counter by 9
4: Increase Poison Counter by 9
5: Increase Poison Counter by 9

---

Ashrana is in Blackfeather Roost on the last isle before entering the Queen chambers.
You say, 'Hail, Ashrana'
Ashrana says 'My sister, Mestle, told me you had agreed to accept our challenge. I'm sure she bored you with threats and doom, but please allow me to tempt you instead. Great riches and treasure can be had at the end of this road if you walk it with me. Are you [interested]?'
You say, 'I am interested'
Ashrana says 'Excellent \_\_\_. The situation is very simple as you'll soon see I'm sure. The fact is that I should be Queen, not my sad and weak sister Eletyl. I aim to [replace] her and assume my rightful role as leader of this Roost. Will you [help] me?'
You say, 'Replace her?'
Ashrana says 'It is far past time for the old to give way to the new and high time I take my rightful place in this roost. Will you [help] me accomplish this and reap the rewards?'
You say, 'I will help you'
Ashrana says 'Your role in this little Drama is quite simple - [Kill] my sister Eletyl, ransack her home and return to me. I will then assume my rightful role and place.'
You say, 'I will kill her'
Ashrana says 'There is one snag to our fine plan - getting to my dear older sister the Queen. You have learned to tame the Griffons of this Roost and move from place to place but a morsel of food will not be enough to accomplish this task. The Queen's abode can only be reached via specially trained Griffons. To ride these Griffons you need the [Royal Crest].'
You say, 'Royal crest?'
Ashrana says 'Captain Slarus leads the Queen's personal guard. Her loyalty to the Queen is unquestioned and so, of course, Slarus must die. But more than that Slarus and her top lieutenants alone have the magical Crest needed to command the Royal Griffons. Kill Slarus and retrieve a Crest for you and your companions. Return to me when you have done so.'
You have been assigned the task 'Blackfeather Roost - Reach the royal throne'.
Captain Slarus leads the Queen's personal guard. Her loyalty to the Queen is unquestioned and so, of course, Slarus must die. But more than Slarus and her top lieutenants alone have the magical Crest needed to command the Royal Griffons. Kill Slarus and retrieve a Crest for you and your companions. Return to me when you have done so.
Kill the Captain of the Guard, Captain Slarus 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
_Captain Slarus can hit for up to 1,700. He has four guards next to him while a fifth guard patrols the area. The trash mobs and guards have a quick respawn time, so you may need to deal with anywhere from 3 to 8 mobs at once to get this kill._
Kill the Royal Guards 0/5 (Blackfeather Roost)
_The guards are clustered around Captain Slarus, close to the nest leading to the Queen's Palace. Neither guards nor the Captain see invis. All drop a Royal Crest. Captain Slarus will drop an item of loot of her own._
Return One Royal Crest to Ashrana 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
Ashrana says 'Aha! Well done \_\_\_! Now we can finally [accomplish] our goal!'
This leads you in to the task 'Kill the Queen of the Harpies!'.
_You are now able to use the griffon nest on the Mesa to reach the Queen's palace which leads to the last task in this series Kill the Queen of the Harpies! [quest=3694]._
**Submitted by:** Lias Roxx, Defiant, Zek

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _There are six islands in Blackfeather Roost, each with its own requirements for access (you can port from island to island after completing a short task for each):_
\- The Base (entered from Goru'kar Mesa)
\- The First Ledge ( see this task [quest=3606])
\- The Harpy Grove ( see this task [quest=3632])
\- The Harpy Cliffs ( see this task [quest=3672])
\- The Harpy Mesa (complete this task)
\- The Royal Throne ( see this task [quest=3695])

---

_Mestle is found near where you're dropped off upon porting up to this island._
You say, 'Hail, Mestle'
Mestle says 'He he! So her [plan] worked I see. Welcome \_\_\_\_\_, welcome. He he!'
You say, 'What plan?'
Mestle says 'Why the plan to bring you here, of course! Didn't you notice how easily you've penetrated into the heart of our Roost? Our slaves Fenith and Coryn each played their part in this drama perfectly. Surely you must know that death lurks around every corner for one such as you, and you must have wondered at the ease you were able to delve deeper and deeper into our home. Now you must choose to [serve] our purpose, or die.'
You say, 'Serve?'
Mestle says 'If you can [prove] yourself worthy to me I will allow you to inch even further into our home. Prove yourself and you will be rewarded. Fail and you will be slain.'
You say, 'I will prove myself'
Mestle says 'There is one here who stands opposed to our purpose, one who must be eliminated for our [strategem] to proceed. If you [accept] my challenge, you will destroy Lady Inthra and prove you are worthy to continue onward and serve us.'
You say, 'Strategem?'
Mestle says 'That is none of your concern until you prove yourself to me! Now, quickly, before I lose my patience -- do you [accept] my challenge?'
You say, 'I accept the challenge'
Mestle says 'Kill her, \_\_\_\_, and return to me when you have done so. Only then will you be permitted to travel deeper into the roost.'
You have been assigned the task 'Blackfeather Roost, The Mesa'.
Do as Mestle...asked...and kill Lady Inthra 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
_Lady Inthra has no protectors and is an easy kill. She is located next to the griffon nest/port-up to the next island._
Return to Mestle and report that the deed is done 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
You say, 'Hail, Mestle'
Your task 'Blackfeather Roost, The Mesa' has been updated.
Mestle says 'Well done, \_\_\_\_. Frail as you seem you may be up to the task ahead of you, if you dare. Here is the griffon food you will need to continue on. Speak with my sister Ashrana, and quickly. She has gone to great effort to bring you this far - do not fail us now or we will turn our fury on you!'
Give the treat to the Griffon on the Cliffs 0/1 (Blackfeather Roost)
_After handing in the treat you can now use the griffon's nest on the Cliffs to reach the Mesa, where you will find Ashrana waiting._
**Submitted by:** Lias Roxx, Defiant, Zek

# Zheren #4: Dyn`Leth's Mine

**Level:** 70 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 70
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo

This is the fourth in a series of five tasks.
Captain Zheren may be found at -1225, +620.
You say, 'Hail, Captain Zheren'
Captain Zheren says '______! Aren't you a sight for sore eyes! We set up this camp a few weeks ago to scout the lands controlled by Dyn`leth. We expected [opposition], but nothing like this.'
You say, 'What opposition?'
Captain Zheren says 'Dyn`leth's armies control almost every piece of land between the mines to the east and the temple to the north. We are vastly outnumbered. Any strength you can lend to our [cause] would be much appreciated.'
You say, 'What cause?'
Captain Zheren says 'We are the Crusade of the Scale! We will not rest until Dyn`leth is dead and the Scale of Veeshan has been reclaimed!'
If you have previously completed "Dyn`Leth's Artillery"..
You have been assigned the task 'Dyn`leth's Mine'.
From the Ashengate Orders, Captain Zheren has learned that Dyn`Leth's forces hope to unearth the ancient dragon Vergalid from the mines below Sunderock. Zheren now wants you to slow the mining operations by any means necessary.
Kill 10 Green Legion Slavedrivers - Sunderock Springs
a Green Legion overseer also counts towards this tally.
Kill Overseer Vorsirus - Sunderock Springs
Can be snared.  Will summon.  Comes with a friend, The Guardian of the Mines, who can also be snared but does not seem to start summoning until the Overseer is dead.
Kill 10 Skullcrush Slaves - Vergalid Mines
Any Skullcrush mob will count.
Kill 10 Kickpick Slaves - Vergalid Mines
Any Kickpick mob will count.
Kill 3 Green Legion Overseers - Vergalid Mines
Report Back to Captain Zheren - Sunderock Springs
You say, 'Hail, Captain Zheren'
Your task 'Dyn`Leth's Mine' has been updated.
You have received a character flag!
Zheren salutes you as you return, 'Welcome back, my friend.  The enemy forces in the area are reeling from the blow you struck to their leaders.  Your deed has been a great service to our [cause]!'
This is a lead-in to the next quest in the series "The Direwind Front".
Your faction standing with Crusade of the Scale got better.
Your faction standing with Forces of Dyn'leth got worse.
Your faction standing with Circle of the Crystalwing got better.
You gain experience!!
Reward Options:
Belt of the Crusader
Shimmering Healer's Mantle
Crusader's Luckstone
Ring of the War Runner
You have successfully been granted your reward for: Dyn`Leth's Mine
Submitted by: Lias Roxx, Defiant, ZekRewards:
- Belt of the Crusader [item=52565]
- Crusader's Luckstone [item=52567]
- Ring of the War Runner [item=52568]
- Shimmering Healer's Mantle [item=52566]

Entered: Sun Oct 8 21:30:15 2006
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | Several Vergalid Mines quests require that you explore the "Ancient Ruins".
The loc for these ruins is 583, -2010. This is in an area reachable only by using a key.
1) Loot an Arid Indicolite Shard. This drops off Kickpick and Skullcrush mobs in the zone.
The Shard is flagged temporary, so don't camp before putting it to use.
2) Go to the Shrine of Zek, blue crystals in the NE of the zone that are also explored as part of the Wanderlust quest. They can be found at 898, -1486.
3) Stand on/at the crystals and right-click your Arid Indicolite Shard. This will give you message "You infuse the crystal's power into Vergalid's life stream."
4) You receive a Flawless Indicolite Shard and can now click on the statue just to your east to reach the inner area of Vergalid Mines.
One person is keyed by this. Just you. However, /corpse can drag people through, and CoH works as well.
**Submitted by:** Lias Roxx, Defiant, Zek
- Flawless Indicolite Shard [item=52550]

# Terraxs's Chain Armor (Part 1)

**Level:** 75 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 75
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This is the outline for armor tasks offered by Terraxs. You will not be able to complete these tasks without an upper-end raiding guild. These tasks result in armor with stats in the neighborhood of 450-475hp/mana/end. After completing one of these tasks, you can request a task in which you add stat bonuses to your armor piece. That outline can be found here.
Find Terraxs on the second floor of Crescent Reach at location -1395, -1920. He is located next to the alchemy vendors.
You say, 'Hail, Terraxs'
Terraxs says 'Vasha, _____. Have you come to me because you hunger for a [challenge]?'
You say, 'I hunger for a challenge'
Terraxs says 'Indeed! I have learned the secret of forming together armors of amazing power but only the strongest and most valiant should attempt to walk the [road] I can lead you down!'
You say, 'A road...'
Terraxs says 'Well not an actual road, no, of course not! I meant it more figuratively! I have discovered the forms of a [recipe] that I think may wish to explore with me, _____!'
You say, 'What recipe is that?'
Terraxs says 'I have compared notes with my friend Tenish about the magical relics we have been coming across. We both have a theory about how to leverage the power lying dormant in these items but Tenish thinks too small for my taste. I have heard that the really powerful relics are closer to the [source] of the magic that has enchanted the relics we have found.'
You say, 'What magic source?'
Terraxs says 'I suppose I should say sources, not source. The peak our cousin Dyn`leth sits atop and the mighty giant enclave both appear to be focal points for incredible energy. If you would like to experiment with me, and if you and your companions are strong enough, then I can share with you my [thoughts] on the subject'
You say, 'Please, share your thoughts'
Terraxs says 'Excellent _____! Return to me when you have gathered these items and we will attempt to craft something fantastic together!'
The following tasks are available, depending on your class (the name of the task is the piece of armor you will receive):
You have been assigned the task '_____'.
Berserkers:
Battlelancer Coat
Battlelancer Leggings
Battlelancer Sleeves
Battlelancer Gauntlets
Battlelancer Boots
Battlelancer Wristguard
Battlelancer Coif
Rangers:
Evergreen Coat
Evergreen Leggings
Evergreen Sleeves
Evergreen Gauntlets
Evergreen Boots
Evergreen Wristguard
Evergreen Coif
Rogues:
Deadkeeper Coat
Deadkeeper Leggings
Deadkeeper Sleeves
Deadkeeper Gauntlets
Deadkeeper Boots
Deadkeeper Wristguard
Deadkeeper Coif
Shamans:
Silverfanged Coat
Silverfanged Leggings
Silverfanged Sleeves
Silverfanged Gauntlets
Silverfanged Boots
Silverfanged Wristguard
Silverfanged Coif
Terraxs believes that he can help you craft new and powerful armors. All you need to do is collect several components and return them to Terraxs so he can construct the final item. Terraxs believes that you should focus your search far to the North.
From what Terraxs can gather there are two types of ancient artifacts that he thinks he can use to construct new armor items.
Ancient artifacts have become infused with a magical coating that looks strangely like Frost. Search along the path to the Giant city we have heard rumors of for such artifacts.
The ash from a distant volcano has fused itself with ancient artifacts, lending strange properties to them. Search along the path to this volcano for these artifacts.
The molds you will need can be found in the same areas as the artifacts although they may be much harder to locate. The local denziens of these areas have become aware of the unique properties of these molds and the most powerful among them are hoarding them for their own purposes. From what Terraxs can gather you may need to face the master of each area to find the molds you seek!
For the Chest Piece:
4x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Chestguard Mold
For the Legs Piece:
4x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Leggings Mold
For the Arms Piece:
3x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Armguard Mold
For the Hands Piece:
2x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Glove Mold
For the Head Piece:
3x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Skullcap Mold
For the Boots Piece:
2x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Boot Mold
For the Wrist Piece:
1x Power Infused Chain Link
1x A Timeworn Bracer Mold
Power Infused Chain Links drop from random trash mobs in Ashengate and Froscrypt raid instances while Timeworn Molds drop from various high-end raid encounters in the same instances.
Give the required items to Terraxs in exchange for your armor piece and completion of the task.
Your task '______' has been updated.
Your task '______' has been updated.
In yellow text:
Well done! I'm impressed at what you accomplished in gathering together the supplies for this item!
You have been given: ______
